Excited for @atproto.nyc atprotonyc



<div class="bsky-image-grid bsky-grid-1" markdown="1">



![Screenshot of claude answering a casual, conversational question about how many people are going to atproto.nyc&#39;s community day, through the Taproot MCP integration.  question: how many are attending the @atproto.nyc &#39;s community day event.  Used Taproot MCP integration · 1 note  answer:  There&#39;s no single &#34;attendee count&#34; published, but the on-network records give a pretty good picture. The event is atproto.nyc community day 2026 — today, actually (Aug 22, 10am–9pm ET at Recurse Center, 397 Bridge Street).  Two different signals:  RSVPs: 122 community.lexicon.calendar.rsvp records from 119 distinct accounts point at the event. That&#39;s the loosest number — the RSVP lexicon has separate &#34;going,&#34; &#34;interested,&#34; and &#34;not going&#34; statuses, and spot-checking shows at least some are just &#34;interested,&#34; so 119 is an upper bound on intent rather than a headcount.  Paid tickets: 86 payment records from 81 distinct accounts. This is the firmer number, since tickets cost money. It&#39;s fuzzy in both directions though — General Admission allows up to 6 tickets per order, so one record can cover several people, while the $50 &#34;Sponsor ticket&#34; is explicitly buying someone else&#39;s seat, so some of those 81 aren&#39;t attending at all.  For context, both ticket types were capped at a quantityTotal of 140, and ticket sales closed at 6:50pm ET today.  So: roughly 80–120 people, with ~81 accounts having actually paid. If you want the exact going/interested split, I can page through all 122 RSVP records and tally the statuses — it&#39;s just a lot of individual record fetches.](https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_fullsize/plain/did:plc:7gm5ejhut7kia2kzglqfew5b/bafkreic7ddkr3zfhquekwe4evzdbqxwzawegbdosuctcsgot7j7b2ukhfm){ .bsky-img }
